     1→import {
     2→  InternalServerErrorException,
     3→  NotFoundException,
     4→  BadRequestException,
     5→} from '@nestjs/common';
     6→import { IpfsProvider } from './ipfs-provider.interface';
     7→
     8→interface PinataResponse {
     9→  IpfsHash: string;
    10→  PinSize: number;
    11→  Timestamp: string;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export class PinataProvider implements IpfsProvider {
    15→  private readonly pinataBaseUrl = 'https://api.pinata.cloud';
    16→  private readonly gatewayBaseUrl = 'https://gateway.pinata.cloud';
    17→
    18→  constructor(private readonly pinataJwt: string) {
    19→    if (!pinataJwt) {
    20→      throw new Error('Pinata JWT is required');
    21→    }
    22→  }
    23→
    24→  /**
    25→   * Pin an encrypted file to IPFS via Pinata.
    26→   * @param data - The encrypted file buffer to pin
    27→   * @param metadata - Optional key-value metadata to attach to the pin
    28→   * @returns The CID and size of the pinned file
    29→   */
    30→  async pinFile(
    31→    data: Buffer,
    32→    metadata?: Record<string, string>
    33→  ): Promise<{ cid: string; size: number }> {
    34→    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
    35→      throw new BadRequestException('File data cannot be empty');
    36→    }
    37→
    38→    // Use native FormData (Node.js 18+) for compatibility with native fetch
    39→    const formData = new FormData();
    40→    // Convert Buffer to ArrayBuffer for Blob compatibility (TypeScript 5.9 strict typing)
    41→    const arrayBuffer = data.buffer.slice(
    42→      data.byteOffset,
    43→      data.byteOffset + data.byteLength
    44→    ) as ArrayBuffer;
    45→    const blob = new Blob([arrayBuffer], { type: 'application/octet-stream' });
    46→    formData.append('file', blob, `encrypted-${Date.now()}`);
    47→
    48→    if (metadata) {
    49→      formData.append(
    50→        'pinataMetadata',
    51→        JSON.stringify({
    52→          keyvalues: metadata,
    53→        })
    54→      );
    55→    }
    56→
    57→    // Always use CIDv1 for modern IPFS
    58→    formData.append(
    59→      'pinataOptions',
    60→      JSON.stringify({
    61→        cidVersion: 1,
    62→      })
    63→    );
    64→
    65→    try {
    66→      const response = await fetch(`${this.pinataBaseUrl}/pinning/pinFileToIPFS`, {
    67→        method: 'POST',
    68→        headers: {
    69→          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.pinataJwt}`,
    70→        },
    71→        body: formData,
    72→      });
    73→
    74→      if (!response.ok) {
    75→        const errorText = await response.text();
    76→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
    77→          `Pinata upload failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
    78→        );
    79→      }
    80→
    81→      const result: PinataResponse = await response.json();
    82→      return {
    83→        cid: result.IpfsHash,
    84→        size: result.PinSize,
    85→      };
    86→    } catch (error) {
    87→      if (error instanceof BadRequestException || error instanceof InternalServerErrorException) {
    88→        throw error;
    89→      }
    90→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
    91→        `Failed to pin file to IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
    92→      );
    93→    }
    94→  }
    95→
    96→  /**
    97→   * Unpin a file from IPFS via Pinata.
    98→   * @param cid - The CID of the file to unpin
    99→   */
   100→  async unpinFile(cid: string): Promise<void> {
   101→    if (!cid || typeof cid !== 'string') {
   102→      throw new BadRequestException('CID is required');
   103→    }
   104→
   105→    try {
   106→      const response = await fetch(`${this.pinataBaseUrl}/pinning/unpin/${cid}`, {
   107→        method: 'DELETE',
   108→        headers: {
   109→          Authorization: `Bearer ${this.pinataJwt}`,
   110→        },
   111→      });
   112→
   113→      // 200 = success, 404 = already unpinned (treat as success)
   114→      if (response.status === 404) {
   115→        // Already unpinned, treat as success
   116→        return;
   117→      }
   118→
   119→      if (!response.ok) {
   120→        const errorText = await response.text();
   121→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   122→          `Pinata unpin failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
   123→        );
   124→      }
   125→    } catch (error) {
   126→      if (
   127→        error instanceof BadRequestException ||
   128→        error instanceof InternalServerErrorException ||
   129→        error instanceof NotFoundException
   130→      ) {
   131→        throw error;
   132→      }
   133→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   134→        `Failed to unpin file from IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
   135→      );
   136→    }
   137→  }
   138→
   139→  /**
   140→   * Get a file from IPFS via Pinata gateway.
   141→   * @param cid - The CID of the file to retrieve
   142→   * @returns The file content as a Buffer
   143→   */
   144→  async getFile(cid: string): Promise<Buffer> {
   145→    if (!cid || typeof cid !== 'string') {
   146→      throw new BadRequestException('CID is required');
   147→    }
   148→
   149→    try {
   150→      const response = await fetch(`${this.gatewayBaseUrl}/ipfs/${cid}`);
   151→
   152→      if (response.status === 404) {
   153→        throw new NotFoundException(`File not found: ${cid}`);
   154→      }
   155→
   156→      if (!response.ok) {
   157→        const errorText = await response.text();
   158→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   159→          `Pinata fetch failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
   160→        );
   161→      }
   162→
   163→      const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
   164→      return Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);
   165→    } catch (error) {
   166→      if (
   167→        error instanceof BadRequestException ||
   168→        error instanceof InternalServerErrorException ||
   169→        error instanceof NotFoundException
   170→      ) {
   171→        throw error;
   172→      }
   173→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   174→        `Failed to get file from IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
   175→      );
   176→    }
   177→  }
   178→}
   179→

     1→import { Injectable, UnauthorizedException } from '@nestjs/common';
     2→import { PassportStrategy } from '@nestjs/passport';
     3→import { ExtractJwt, Strategy } from 'passport-jwt';
     4→import { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
     5→import { InjectRepository } from '@nestjs/typeorm';
     6→import { Repository } from 'typeorm';
     7→import { User } from '../entities/user.entity';
     8→
     9→interface JwtPayload {
    10→  sub: string; // User ID (UUID)
    11→  publicKey: string;
    12→  iat: number;
    13→  exp: number;
    14→}
    15→
    16→@Injectable()
    17→export class JwtStrategy extends PassportStrategy(Strategy) {
    18→  constructor(
    19→    configService: ConfigService,
    20→    @InjectRepository(User)
    21→    private userRepository: Repository<User>
    22→  ) {
    23→    const secret = configService.get<string>('JWT_SECRET');
    24→    if (!secret) {
    25→      throw new Error('JWT_SECRET environment variable is not set');
    26→    }
    27→    super({
    28→      jwtFromRequest: ExtractJwt.fromAuthHeaderAsBearerToken(),
    29→      ignoreExpiration: false,
    30→      secretOrKey: secret,
    31→    });
    32→  }
    33→
    34→  async validate(payload: JwtPayload): Promise<User> {
    35→    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
    36→      where: { id: payload.sub },
    37→    });
    38→
    39→    if (!user) {
    40→      throw new UnauthorizedException('User not found');
    41→    }
    42→
    43→    return user;
    44→  }
    45→}
    46→

await (async (page) => {
  // Intercept the next /ipfs/upload response
  const responsePromise = page.waitForResponse(resp => resp.url().includes('/ipfs/upload'));
  
  // Trigger upload
  const [fileChooser] = await Promise.all([
    page.waitForEvent('filechooser'),
    page.locator('.upload-zone').first().click()
  ]);
  await fileChooser.setFiles('/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/cipherbox-test.txt');
  
  const response = await responsePromise;
  const body = await response.text();
  return { status: response.status(), body };
})(page);
```

### Ran Playwright code
```js
await (async (page) => {
  // Intercept the request to capture the auth header and response body
  let authHeader = '';
  let responseBody = '';
  let responseStatus = 0;
  
  await page.route('**/ipfs/upload', async route => {
    authHeader = route.request().headers()['authorization'] || 'none';
    const response = await route.fetch();
    responseStatus = response.status();
    responseBody = (await response.body()).toString();
    await route.fulfill({ response });
  });
  
  // Trigger upload
  const [fileChooser] = await Promise.all([
    page.waitForEvent('filechooser'),
    page.locator('.upload-zone').first().click()
  ]);
  await fileChooser.setFiles('/Users/michael/Code/cipher-box/cipherbox-test.txt');
  
  // Wait for the request
  await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
  
  // Clean up route
  await page.unroute('**/ipfs/upload');
  
  return { authHeader: authHeader.substring(0, 30) + '...', responseStatus, responseBody };
})(page);
```

     1→import {

     5→} from '@nestjs/common';
     6→import { IpfsProvider } from './ipfs-provider.interface';
     7→
     8→interface KuboAddResponse {
     9→  Name: string;
    10→  Hash: string;
    11→  Size: string;
    12→}
    13→
    14→export class LocalProvider implements IpfsProvider {
    15→  constructor(
    16→    private readonly apiUrl: string,
    17→    // Gateway URL kept for future use (e.g., public read access)
    18→    // Currently using API directly via cat endpoint
    19→    _gatewayUrl: string
    20→  ) {
    21→    if (!apiUrl) {
    22→      throw new Error('IPFS API URL is required');
    23→    }
    24→  }
    25→
    26→  /**
    27→   * Pin a file to local IPFS node via Kubo API.
    28→   * @param data - The file buffer to pin
    29→   * @param metadata - Optional metadata (ignored for local IPFS)
    30→   * @returns The CID and size of the pinned file
    31→   */
    32→  async pinFile(
    33→    data: Buffer,
    34→    _metadata?: Record<string, string>
    35→  ): Promise<{ cid: string; size: number }> {
    36→    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
    37→      throw new BadRequestException('File data cannot be empty');
    38→    }
    39→
    40→    // Use native FormData (available in Node.js 18+) with Blob for compatibility with native fetch
    41→    const formData = new FormData();
    42→    // Create Blob from Buffer using explicit Uint8Array conversion for type safety
    43→    const blob = new Blob([new Uint8Array(data)], { type: 'application/octet-stream' });
    44→    formData.append('file', blob, `file-${Date.now()}`);
    45→
    46→    try {
    47→      // Kubo API uses POST for all operations
    48→      // pin=true ensures the file is pinned, cid-version=1 for CIDv1
    49→      const response = await fetch(`${this.apiUrl}/api/v0/add?pin=true&cid-version=1`, {
    50→        method: 'POST',
    51→        body: formData,
    52→      });
    53→
    54→      if (!response.ok) {
    55→        const errorText = await response.text();
    56→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
    57→          `IPFS add failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
    58→        );
    59→      }
    60→
    61→      const result: KuboAddResponse = await response.json();
    62→      return {
    63→        cid: result.Hash,
    64→        size: parseInt(result.Size, 10),
    65→      };
    66→    } catch (error) {
    67→      if (error instanceof BadRequestException || error instanceof InternalServerErrorException) {
    68→        throw error;
    69→      }
    70→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
    71→        `Failed to pin file to IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
    72→      );
    73→    }
    74→  }
    75→
    76→  /**
    77→   * Unpin a file from local IPFS node.
    78→   * @param cid - The CID of the file to unpin
    79→   */
    80→  async unpinFile(cid: string): Promise<void> {
    81→    if (!cid || typeof cid !== 'string') {
    82→      throw new BadRequestException('CID is required');
    83→    }
    84→
    85→    try {
    86→      // Kubo API uses POST for all operations including unpin
    87→      const response = await fetch(`${this.apiUrl}/api/v0/pin/rm?arg=${cid}`, {
    88→        method: 'POST',
    89→      });
    90→
    91→      if (!response.ok) {
    92→        const errorText = await response.text();
    93→        // "not pinned" means already unpinned - treat as success (idempotent)
    94→        if (errorText.includes('not pinned')) {
    95→          return;
    96→        }
    97→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
    98→          `IPFS unpin failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
    99→        );
   100→      }
   101→    } catch (error) {
   102→      if (
   103→        error instanceof BadRequestException ||
   104→        error instanceof InternalServerErrorException ||
   105→        error instanceof NotFoundException
   106→      ) {
   107→        throw error;
   108→      }
   109→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   110→        `Failed to unpin file from IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
   111→      );
   112→    }
   113→  }
   114→
   115→  /**
   116→   * Get a file from local IPFS node via Kubo API.
   117→   * @param cid - The CID of the file to retrieve
   118→   * @returns The file content as a Buffer
   119→   */
   120→  async getFile(cid: string): Promise<Buffer> {
   121→    if (!cid || typeof cid !== 'string') {
   122→      throw new BadRequestException('CID is required');
   123→    }
   124→
   125→    try {
   126→      // Kubo API uses POST for cat operation
   127→      const response = await fetch(`${this.apiUrl}/api/v0/cat?arg=${cid}`, {
   128→        method: 'POST',
   129→      });
   130→
   131→      if (!response.ok) {
   132→        const errorText = await response.text();
   133→        // Check for not found errors

   135→          errorText.includes('not found') ||
   136→          errorText.includes('no link') ||
   137→          errorText.includes('blockstore: block not found')
   138→        ) {
   139→          throw new NotFoundException(`File not found: ${cid}`);
   140→        }
   141→        throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   142→          `IPFS cat failed: ${response.status} - ${errorText}`
   143→        );
   144→      }
   145→
   146→      const arrayBuffer = await response.arrayBuffer();
   147→      return Buffer.from(arrayBuffer);
   148→    } catch (error) {
   149→      if (
   150→        error instanceof BadRequestException ||
   151→        error instanceof InternalServerErrorException ||
   152→        error instanceof NotFoundException
   153→      ) {

   155→      }
   156→      throw new InternalServerErrorException(
   157→        `Failed to get file from IPFS: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Unknown error'}`
   158→      );
   159→    }
   160→  }
   161→}

     2→import { ConfigModule, ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
     3→import { IPFS_PROVIDER, IpfsProvider, PinataProvider, LocalProvider } from './providers';

     7→@Module({})
     8→export class IpfsModule {
     9→  static forRootAsync(): DynamicModule {

    11→      module: IpfsModule,

    17→          useFactory: (configService: ConfigService): IpfsProvider => {

> AGENT

The store is purely in-memory — no persistence. The email is not sensitive data, so the simplest fix is to read/write `userEmail` to localStorage. I'll persist it on set and hydrate it on store creation, and clear it on logout.

> AGENT

That's it. Now `userEmail` is:
- **Hydrated from localStorage** on store creation (survives refresh/session restore)
- **Persisted to localStorage** when set during login
- **Cleared from localStorage** on logout

The email isn't sensitive data, so this is safe — unlike tokens or keys which stay memory-only.

    41→@ApiTags('Auth')
    42→@Controller('auth')
    43→export class AuthController {
    44→  constructor(private authService: AuthService) {}
    45→
    46→  @Post('login')
    47→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
    48→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Authenticate user with Web3Auth ID token' })
    49→  @ApiResponse({
    50→    status: 200,
    51→    description: 'Successfully authenticated',
    52→    type: LoginResponseDto,
    53→  })
    54→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Invalid Web3Auth token' })
    55→  async login(
    56→    @Body() loginDto: LoginDto,
    57→    @Req() req: ExpressRequest,
    58→    @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response
    59→  ): Promise<LoginResponseDto> {
    60→    const result = await this.authService.login(loginDto);
    61→    const isDesktop = req.headers['x-client-type'] === 'desktop';
    62→
    63→    if (isDesktop) {
    64→      // Desktop clients: return refreshToken in response body (no cookie)
    65→      return {
    66→        accessToken: result.accessToken,
    67→        refreshToken: result.refreshToken,
    68→        isNewUser: result.isNewUser,
    69→      };
    70→    }
    71→
    72→    // Web clients: set refresh token in HTTP-only cookie
    73→    res.cookie('refresh_token', result.refreshToken, REFRESH_TOKEN_COOKIE_OPTIONS);
    74→
    75→    return {
    76→      accessToken: result.accessToken,
    77→      isNewUser: result.isNewUser,
    78→    };

    89→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Invalid or expired refresh token' })
    90→  async refresh(

   102→    } else {
   103→      // Web clients: read refresh token from cookie
   104→      refreshToken = req.cookies?.['refresh_token'];
   105→    }
   106→
   107→    if (!refreshToken) {
   108→      throw new UnauthorizedException('No refresh token');
   109→    }
   110→
   111→    const result = await this.authService.refreshByToken(refreshToken);
   112→
   113→    if (isDesktop) {
   114→      // Desktop clients: return new refreshToken in response body (no cookie)
   115→      return {
   116→        accessToken: result.accessToken,
   117→        refreshToken: result.refreshToken,
   118→      };
   119→    }
   120→
   121→    // Web clients: set new refresh token in HTTP-only cookie (rotation)
   122→    res.cookie('refresh_token', result.refreshToken, REFRESH_TOKEN_COOKIE_OPTIONS);
   123→
   124→    return {
   125→      accessToken: result.accessToken,
   126→    };
   127→  }
   128→
   129→  @Post('logout')
   130→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
   131→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
   132→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   133→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Logout user and invalidate all refresh tokens' })
   134→  @ApiResponse({
   135→    status: 200,
   136→    description: 'Successfully logged out',
   137→    type: LogoutResponseDto,
   138→  })
   139→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   140→  async logout(
   141→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   142→    @Res({ passthrough: true }) res: Response
   143→  ): Promise<LogoutResponseDto> {
   144→    const isDesktop = (req as unknown as ExpressRequest).headers['x-client-type'] === 'desktop';
   145→
   146→    if (!isDesktop) {
   147→      // Web clients: clear the refresh token cookie
   148→      res.clearCookie('refresh_token', { path: '/auth' });
   149→    }
   150→
   151→    return this.authService.logout(req.user.id);
   152→  }
   153→
   154→  @Get('methods')
   155→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
   156→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   157→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get all linked auth methods for the current user' })
   158→  @ApiResponse({
   159→    status: 200,
   160→    description: 'List of linked auth methods',
   161→    type: [AuthMethodResponseDto],
   162→  })
   163→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   164→  async getMethods(@Request() req: RequestWithUser): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   165→    return this.authService.getLinkedMethods(req.user.id);
   166→  }
   167→
   168→  @Post('link')
   169→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
   170→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
   171→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   172→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Link a new auth method to the current user account' })
   173→  @ApiResponse({
   174→    status: 200,
   175→    description: 'Auth method linked successfully, returns updated list of methods',
   176→    type: [AuthMethodResponseDto],
   177→  })
   178→  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Auth method already linked or publicKey mismatch' })
   179→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   180→  async linkMethod(
   181→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   182→    @Body() linkDto: LinkMethodDto
   183→  ): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   184→    return this.authService.linkMethod(req.user.id, linkDto);
   185→  }
   186→
   187→  @Post('unlink')
   188→  @HttpCode(HttpStatus.OK)
   189→  @UseGuards(JwtAuthGuard)
   190→  @ApiBearerAuth()
   191→  @ApiOperation({ summary: 'Unlink an auth method from the current user account' })
   192→  @ApiResponse({
   193→    status: 200,
   194→    description: 'Auth method unlinked successfully',
   195→    type: UnlinkMethodResponseDto,
   196→  })
   197→  @ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Cannot unlink last auth method or method not found' })
   198→  @ApiResponse({ status: 401, description: 'Unauthorized' })
   199→  async unlinkMethod(
   200→    @Request() req: RequestWithUser,
   201→    @Body() unlinkDto: UnlinkMethodDto
   202→  ): Promise<UnlinkMethodResponseDto> {
   203→    await this.authService.unlinkMethod(req.user.id, unlinkDto.methodId);
   204→    return { success: true };
   205→  }

    32→  async login(loginDto: LoginDto): Promise<LoginServiceResult> {

    43→    if (loginDto.loginType === 'corekit') {
    44→      // Core Kit login: verify the CipherBox-issued JWT using our own key.
    45→      // The client already authenticated via /auth/identity/* endpoints,
    46→      // received a CipherBox JWT, used it for Core Kit loginWithJWT,
    47→      // and now sends it here for backend session creation.
    48→      payload = await this.verifyCipherBoxJwt(loginDto.idToken);
    49→    } else {
    50→      // PnP / external wallet: verify Web3Auth-issued JWT
    51→      const verificationKey =
    52→        loginDto.loginType === 'external_wallet' && loginDto.walletAddress
    53→          ? loginDto.walletAddress
    54→          : loginDto.publicKey;
    55→
    56→      payload = await this.web3AuthVerifier.verifyIdToken(
    57→        loginDto.idToken,
    58→        verificationKey,
    59→        loginDto.loginType
    60→      );
    61→    }
    62→
    63→    // 2. Find or create user
    64→    let user = await this.userRepository.findOne({
    65→      where: { publicKey: loginDto.publicKey },
    66→    });
    67→
    68→    // 2b. Placeholder publicKey resolution for Core Kit identity provider.
    69→    // When a user first authenticates via CipherBox identity provider (Plan 12-01),
    70→    // they get a placeholder publicKey ('pending-core-kit-{userId}').
    71→    // After Core Kit login, the client calls /auth/login with the REAL publicKey.

    73→    if (!user) {

    77→          where: { publicKey: Like(`pending-core-kit-${verifierId}%`) },
    78→        });

    80→          this.logger.log(`Resolving placeholder publicKey for user ${placeholderUser.id}`);
    81→          placeholderUser.publicKey = loginDto.publicKey;

    84→      }

    91→    const isNewUser = !user;

    96→      });
    97→    } else if (user.derivationVersion !== derivationVersion) {

   120→    if (!authMethod) {

   127→
   128→    // 4. Update last used timestamp

   135→    return {

   141→
   142→  /**
   143→   * Verify a CipherBox-issued JWT for Core Kit login flow.
   144→   * Since we are the identity provider, we verify against our own JWKS.
   145→   */
   146→  private async verifyCipherBoxJwt(
   147→    idToken: string
   148→  ): Promise<{ sub?: string; verifierId?: string }> {
   149→    try {
   150→      const jwksData = this.jwtIssuerService.getJwksData();
   151→      const jwks = jose.createLocalJWKSet(jwksData);
   152→      const { payload } = await jose.jwtVerify(idToken, jwks, {
   153→        issuer: 'cipherbox',
   154→        audience: 'web3auth',
   155→        algorithms: ['RS256'],
   156→      });
   157→      return { sub: payload.sub, verifierId: payload.sub };
   158→    } catch (error) {
   159→      throw new UnauthorizedException(
   160→        `Invalid CipherBox identity token: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'verification failed'}`
   161→      );
   162→    }
   163→  }
   164→
   165→  async refresh(
   166→    refreshToken: string,
   167→    userId: string,
   168→    publicKey: string
   169→  ): Promise<RefreshServiceResult> {
   170→    const tokens = await this.tokenService.rotateRefreshToken(refreshToken, userId, publicKey);
   171→
   172→    return {
   173→      accessToken: tokens.accessToken,
   174→      refreshToken: tokens.refreshToken,
   175→    };
   176→  }
   177→
   178→  async logout(userId: string): Promise<LogoutResponseDto> {
   179→    await this.tokenService.revokeAllUserTokens(userId);
   180→    return { success: true };
   181→  }
   182→
   183→  /**
   184→   * Refresh tokens by searching for the matching refresh token across all users.
   185→   * This allows refresh without requiring the (possibly expired) access token.
   186→   */
   187→  async refreshByToken(refreshToken: string): Promise<RefreshServiceResult> {
   188→    // Find candidate tokens by prefix for O(1) lookup instead of O(N) Argon2 scan
   189→    const prefix = refreshToken.substring(0, 16);
   190→    const tokens = await this.refreshTokenRepository.find({
   191→      where: {
   192→        tokenPrefix: prefix,
   193→        revokedAt: IsNull(),
   194→      },
   195→      relations: ['user'],
   196→    });
   197→
   198→    // Find matching token by verifying against hashes
   199→    let validToken: RefreshToken | null = null;
   200→    for (const token of tokens) {
   201→      // Skip expired tokens
   202→      if (token.expiresAt < new Date()) {
   203→        continue;
   204→      }
   205→      try {
   206→        if (await argon2.verify(token.tokenHash, refreshToken)) {
   207→          validToken = token;
   208→          break;
   209→        }
   210→      } catch {
   211→        // argon2.verify throws on invalid hash format, continue checking
   212→        continue;
   213→      }
   214→    }
   215→
   216→    if (!validToken) {
   217→      throw new UnauthorizedException('Invalid or expired refresh token');
   218→    }
   219→
   220→    // Revoke old token
   221→    validToken.revokedAt = new Date();
   222→    await this.refreshTokenRepository.save(validToken);
   223→
   224→    // Create new tokens
   225→    const newTokens = await this.tokenService.createTokens(
   226→      validToken.userId,
   227→      validToken.user.publicKey
   228→    );
   229→
   230→    return {
   231→      accessToken: newTokens.accessToken,
   232→      refreshToken: newTokens.refreshToken,
   233→    };
   234→  }
   235→
   236→  /**
   237→   * Get all linked auth methods for a user.
   238→   */
   239→  async getLinkedMethods(userId: string): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   240→    const methods = await this.authMethodRepository.find({
   241→      where: { userId },
   242→      order: { createdAt: 'ASC' },
   243→    });
   244→
   245→    return methods.map((method) => ({
   246→      id: method.id,
   247→      type: method.type,
   248→      identifier: method.identifier,
   249→      lastUsedAt: method.lastUsedAt,
   250→      createdAt: method.createdAt,
   251→    }));
   252→  }
   253→
   254→  /**
   255→   * Link a new auth method to an existing user account.
   256→   * CRITICAL: The new auth method's publicKey must match the user's publicKey
   257→   * (ensuring both auth methods derive the same keypair via Web3Auth group connections)
   258→   */
   259→  async linkMethod(userId: string, linkDto: LinkMethodDto): Promise<AuthMethodResponseDto[]> {
   260→    // 1. Get the user to find their publicKey
   261→    const user = await this.userRepository.findOne({ where: { id: userId } });
   262→    if (!user) {
   263→      throw new UnauthorizedException('User not found');
   264→    }
   265→
   266→    // 2. Verify the new idToken with Web3AuthVerifierService
   267→    // This also validates that the new token's publicKey matches the user's publicKey
   268→    const payload = await this.web3AuthVerifier.verifyIdToken(
   269→      linkDto.idToken,
   270→      user.publicKey,
   271→      linkDto.loginType
   272→    );
   273→
   274→    // 3. Extract type and identifier from token payload
   275→    const authMethodType = this.web3AuthVerifier.extractAuthMethodType(payload, linkDto.loginType);
   276→    const identifier = this.web3AuthVerifier.extractIdentifier(payload);
   277→
   278→    // 4. Check if this exact method (type + identifier) is already linked
   279→    const existingMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   280→      where: {
   281→        userId,
   282→        type: authMethodType,
   283→        identifier,
   284→      },
   285→    });
   286→
   287→    if (existingMethod) {
   288→      throw new BadRequestException('This auth method is already linked to your account');
   289→    }
   290→
   291→    // 5. Create new AuthMethod entity
   292→    await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   293→      userId,
   294→      type: authMethodType,
   295→      identifier,
   296→      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   297→    });
   298→
   299→    // 6. Return updated list of methods
   300→    return this.getLinkedMethods(userId);
   301→  }
   302→
   303→  /**
   304→   * Unlink an auth method from a user account.
   305→   * Cannot unlink the last remaining auth method.
   306→   */
   307→  async unlinkMethod(userId: string, methodId: string): Promise<void> {
   308→    // 1. Find the method by id and userId
   309→    const method = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   310→      where: { id: methodId, userId },
   311→    });
   312→
   313→    if (!method) {
   314→      throw new BadRequestException('Auth method not found');
   315→    }
   316→
   317→    // 2. Count remaining methods for user
   318→    const methodCount = await this.authMethodRepository.count({
   319→      where: { userId },
   320→    });
   321→
   322→    // 3. Cannot unlink if only 1 method remains
   323→    if (methodCount <= 1) {
   324→      throw new BadRequestException('Cannot unlink your last auth method');
   325→    }
   326→
   327→    // 4. Delete the method
   328→    await this.authMethodRepository.remove(method);
   329→  }
   330→}

> AGENT

API recompiled and restarted cleanly. And IPNS even resolved successfully this time. Here's a summary of the changes:

**Backend:**
- `auth.service.ts`: `refreshByToken` now queries the user's `email_passwordless` auth method and returns the email
- `auth.controller.ts`: Passes `email` through in the refresh response (both web and desktop)
- `dto/token.dto.ts`: Added optional `email` field to `TokenResponseDto` and `RefreshServiceResult`

**Frontend:**
- `lib/api/auth.ts`: Added `email?: string` to `TokenResponse` type
- `hooks/useAuth.ts`: Sets `userEmail` from refresh response during session restore
- `components/layout/UserMenu.tsx`: Fallback changed from `'User'` to `'[an0n]'`

Now on session refresh, the backend returns the email from the auth method table — no PII stored locally, and `[an0n]` shows when no email is available (e.g. Google login).

     1→import { Injectable, Logger, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
     2→import { ConfigService } from '@nestjs/config';
     3→import * as jose from 'jose';
     4→
     5→const KID = 'cipherbox-identity-1';
     6→/** RSA private JWK fields to strip when creating the public JWK */
     7→const RSA_PRIVATE_FIELDS = new Set(['d', 'p', 'q', 'dp', 'dq', 'qi']);
     8→
     9→@Injectable()
    10→export class JwtIssuerService implements OnModuleInit {
    11→  private readonly logger = new Logger(JwtIssuerService.name);
    12→  private privateKey!: jose.CryptoKey | jose.KeyObject;
    13→  private publicJwk!: jose.JWK;
    14→
    15→  constructor(private config: ConfigService) {}
    16→
    17→  async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
    18→    const pemKey = this.config.get<string>('IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY');
    19→
    20→    if (pemKey) {
    21→      this.logger.log('Loading RS256 identity keypair from IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY env var');
    22→      this.privateKey = await jose.importPKCS8(pemKey, 'RS256');
    23→      // Derive public JWK from private key (strip private fields)
    24→      const privateJwk = await jose.exportJWK(this.privateKey);
    25→      const publicJwkData = Object.fromEntries(
    26→        Object.entries(privateJwk).filter(([k]) => !RSA_PRIVATE_FIELDS.has(k))
    27→      );
    28→      this.publicJwk = { ...publicJwkData, kid: KID, alg: 'RS256', use: 'sig' };
    29→    } else {
    30→      this.logger.warn(
    31→        'IDENTITY_JWT_PRIVATE_KEY not set — generating ephemeral RS256 keypair (dev/staging only)'
    32→      );
    33→      const { publicKey, privateKey } = await jose.generateKeyPair('RS256', {
    34→        modulusLength: 2048,
    35→      });
    36→      this.privateKey = privateKey;
    37→      const jwk = await jose.exportJWK(publicKey);
    38→      this.publicJwk = { ...jwk, kid: KID, alg: 'RS256', use: 'sig' };
    39→    }
    40→
    41→    this.logger.log(`Identity JWKS ready (kid=${KID})`);
    42→  }
    43→
    44→  /**
    45→   * Sign a CipherBox identity JWT for Web3Auth custom verifier consumption.
    46→   *
    47→   * Claims: iss=cipherbox, aud=web3auth, sub=userId, exp=5min
    48→   */
    49→  async signIdentityJwt(userId: string): Promise<string> {
    50→    return new jose.SignJWT({ sub: userId })
    51→      .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: KID })
    52→      .setIssuer('cipherbox')
    53→      .setAudience('web3auth')
    54→      .setIssuedAt()
    55→      .setExpirationTime('5m')
    56→      .sign(this.privateKey);
    57→  }
    58→
    59→  /**
    60→   * Return JWKS data for the /.well-known/jwks.json endpoint.
    61→   */
    62→  getJwksData(): { keys: jose.JWK[] } {
    63→    return { keys: [this.publicJwk] };
    64→  }
    65→}
    66→

> AGENT

I see the full picture now. The identity controller at line 97 calls `signIdentityJwt(user.id)` — email is available there but not included in the JWT. Two-part fix:

1. Add `email` claim to the identity JWT
2. Extract it in `verifyCipherBoxJwt` and use it as the auth method identifier

  "old_string": "  /**\n   * Sign a CipherBox identity JWT for Web3Auth custom verifier consumption.\n   *\n   * Claims: iss=cipherbox, aud=web3auth, sub=userId, exp=5min\n   */\n  async signIdentityJwt(userId: string): Promise<string> {\n    return new jose.SignJWT({ sub: userId })\n      .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: KID })\n      .setIssuer('cipherbox')\n      .setAudience('web3auth')\n      .setIssuedAt()\n      .setExpirationTime('5m')\n      .sign(this.privateKey);\n  }",
  "new_string": "  /**\n   * Sign a CipherBox identity JWT for Web3Auth custom verifier consumption.\n   *\n   * Claims: iss=cipherbox, aud=web3auth, sub=userId, email (optional), exp=5min\n   */\n  async signIdentityJwt(userId: string, email?: string): Promise<string> {\n    const claims: Record<string, unknown> = { sub: userId };\n    if (email) {\n      claims.email = email;\n    }\n    return new jose.SignJWT(claims)\n      .setProtectedHeader({ alg: 'RS256', kid: KID })\n      .setIssuer('cipherbox')\n      .setAudience('web3auth')\n      .setIssuedAt()\n      .setExpirationTime('5m')\n      .sign(this.privateKey);\n  }"
}

   111→  private async findOrCreateUserByEmail(

   114→  ): Promise<{ user: User; isNewUser: boolean }> {
   115→    const normalizedEmail = email.toLowerCase().trim();
   116→
   117→    // Look for existing auth method with this email
   118→    const existingMethod = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   119→      where: {
   120→        type: authMethodType,
   121→        identifier: normalizedEmail,
   122→      },
   123→      relations: ['user'],
   124→    });
   125→
   126→    if (existingMethod) {
   127→      // Update last used timestamp
   128→      existingMethod.lastUsedAt = new Date();
   129→      await this.authMethodRepository.save(existingMethod);
   130→      return { user: existingMethod.user, isNewUser: false };
   131→    }
   132→
   133→    // Also check if this email exists under any auth method type
   134→    // (e.g., user signed up with Google, now logging in with email)
   135→    const anyMethodWithEmail = await this.authMethodRepository.findOne({
   136→      where: { identifier: normalizedEmail },
   137→      relations: ['user'],
   138→    });
   139→
   140→    if (anyMethodWithEmail) {
   141→      // Same email, different auth method -- link to existing user
   142→      await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   143→        userId: anyMethodWithEmail.user.id,
   144→        type: authMethodType,
   145→        identifier: normalizedEmail,
   146→        lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   147→      });
   148→      this.logger.log(
   149→        `Linked ${authMethodType} to existing user ${anyMethodWithEmail.user.id} via email ${normalizedEmail}`
   150→      );
   151→      return { user: anyMethodWithEmail.user, isNewUser: false };
   152→    }
   153→
   154→    // Create new user with placeholder publicKey
   155→    const newUser = await this.userRepository.save({
   156→      publicKey: `pending-core-kit-${Date.now()}`,
   157→    });
   158→
   159→    // Create auth method
   160→    await this.authMethodRepository.save({
   161→      userId: newUser.id,
   162→      type: authMethodType,
   163→      identifier: normalizedEmail,
   164→      lastUsedAt: new Date(),
   165→    });
   166→
   167→    return { user: newUser, isNewUser: true };
   168→  }
   169→}
   170→
```

> AGENT

I see — the identity controller's `findOrCreateUserByEmail` already stores the email as the `identifier` in the auth method. The problem is that `auth.service.ts` login creates a **second** auth method with `identifier: payload.sub` (UUID). So there are two auth methods for the same user — one with email (from identity flow), one with UUID (from login flow).

The fix is simpler than I thought: just include the email in the JWT so the login flow can use it as the identifier instead of the UUID.

> AGENT

Actually, for the initial login flow the frontend already has the email from the form input and sets it via `setUserEmail`. The refresh response is the only place it's missing. The changes I've already made should be sufficient — the `refreshByToken` now queries the auth method which will have the actual email as identifier (instead of UUID) going forward.

But there's one issue: the existing user's auth method still has the UUID as identifier from before this fix. Let me also make the `refreshByToken` email lookup more robust — also check the `email` claim from the identity JWT if the auth method has a UUID-looking identifier. Actually, the simpler approach: since the identity controller already created an auth method with the real email, the `refreshByToken` lookup will find it. The duplicate auth method with UUID as identifier was created by the login flow — that won't happen anymore. And for existing users, the identity-created auth method with the real email still exists.

> AGENT

API compiled cleanly. Summary of what changed:

1. **JWT issuer** now includes `email` claim in identity JWTs
2. **Identity controller** passes email to `signIdentityJwt` for both Google and email OTP flows
3. **Auth service** extracts email from JWT and uses it as the auth method identifier (falling back to `sub` if no email)
4. **Refresh endpoint** returns the email from the user's `email_passwordless` auth method
5. **Frontend** sets `userEmail` from refresh response during session restore
6. **UserMenu** shows `[an0n]` when no email is available

You'll need to log in fresh for the email to be stored correctly in the auth method (the old auth method still has the UUID). Want to test it?
